2. Black has several attacking pieces positioned to create threats
3. There's tactical opportunity on the e-file

My analysis:
- e7e8 would create a passed pawn and restrict white
- e7f8 (Qxf8+) would give check and force white to defend
- g5h4 creates threats on the kingside

The most principled move is **e7e8** - advancing the pawn creates pressure and free space for Black's pieces. However, **e7f8** is more aggressive, giving immediate check with Qxf8+ which forces White's hand.

I'll go with **e7e8** to build long-term pressure:
